&lt;parameter name="content"&gt;/* PBIPatika — dashboard çekirdeği: dilimleyiciler, çapraz filtre, detaya inme, RLS benzetimi ve görsellere verilen API. */
(function (W) {
'use strict';
const X = W.PBI_DAX, V = W.PBIViz, MODEL = W.PBI_MODEL;
const pc = s =&gt; { const m = String(s).match(/^(\w+)\[(.+)\]$/); return m ? { t: m[1], c: m[2] } : null; };
const vals = (t, c) =&gt; [...new Set(MODEL.tables[t].rows.map(r =&gt; r[c]).filter(v =&gt; v != null))].sort((a, b) =&gt; X.sortCompare({ t, c }, a, b));
const repIds = [...new Set(MODEL.tables.Satislar.rows.map(r =&gt; r.TemsilciNo))];
const reps = MODEL.tables.Calisanlar.rows.filter(r =&gt; repIds.includes(r.CalisanNo) &amp;&amp; !/Müdür/.test(r.Unvan)).map(r =&gt; r.AdSoyad);
const pct = v =&gt; (v &gt; 0 ? '+' : v &lt; 0 ? '−' : '') + Math.round(Math.abs(v) * 100) + '%';

/* Dilimleyici tanımları. single: tek seçim; req: boş bırakılamaz (parametre); rls: filtre değil rol */
const SL = {
  yil: { t: 'Tarih', c: 'Yil', label: 'Yıl', vals: vals('Tarih', 'Yil'), def: [2024] },
  yilMulti: { t: 'Tarih', c: 'Yil', label: 'Yıl (Ctrl ile çoklu)', vals: vals('Tarih', 'Yil'), def: [2023, 2024] },
  kanal: { t: 'Satislar', c: 'Kanal', label: 'Kanal', vals: ['Web', 'Mobil', 'Mağaza'], def: [] },
  segment: { t: 'Musteriler', c: 'Segment', label: 'Segment', vals: vals('Musteriler', 'Segment'), def: [] },
  kategori: { t: 'Kategoriler', c: 'Kategori', label: 'Kategori', vals: vals('Kategoriler', 'Kategori'), def: [], style: 'dropdown' },
  topn: { t: 'TopNSecim', c: 'N', label: 'İlk N (parametre)', vals: vals('TopNSecim', 'N'), def: [5], single: true, req: true, style: 'dropdown' },
  fiyat: { t: 'FiyatSenaryosu', c: 'FiyatDegisimi', label: 'Fiyat değişimi (ne olur parametresi)', vals: vals('FiyatSenaryosu', 'FiyatDegisimi'), def: [0], single: true, req: true, fmtv: v =&gt; v === 0 ? '0' : pct(v) },
  para: { t: 'ParaBirimleri', c: 'ParaBirimi', label: 'Para birimi', vals: ['TRY', 'USD', 'EUR'], def: ['TRY'], single: true, req: true },
  viewAs: { t: 'Calisanlar', c: 'AdSoyad', label: 'Farklı görüntüle (RLS rolü: Temsilci)', vals: reps, def: [], single: true, rls: true, style: 'dropdown' },
  cihaz: { t: 'WebOlaylari', c: 'Cihaz', label: 'Cihaz', vals: vals('WebOlaylari', 'Cihaz'), def: [] },
  kaynak: { t: 'WebOlaylari', c: 'Kaynak', label: 'Trafik kaynağı', vals: vals('WebOlaylari', 'Kaynak'), def: [], style: 'dropdown' },
  departman: { t: 'Departmanlar', c: 'Departman', label: 'Departman', vals: vals('Departmanlar', 'Departman'), def: [], style: 'dropdown' }
};

function initState(dash) {
  const sl = {}; (dash.slicers || []).forEach(k =&gt; { sl[k] = (SL[k] ? SL[k].def : []).slice(); });
  return { id: dash.id, sl, cross: null, drill: {} };
}
/* dilimleyiciye tıklama: ctrl = çoklu seçim (Power BI gibi); yıl dilimleyicileri dokunmatikte de çoklu */
function toggleSlicer(st, key, v, multi) {
  const d = SL[key]; const cur = st.sl[key] || [];
  if (d.single) { st.sl[key] = cur.length === 1 &amp;&amp; X.keyOf(cur[0]) === X.keyOf(v) &amp;&amp; !d.req ? [] : [v]; return; }
  const has = cur.some(x =&gt; X.keyOf(x) === X.keyOf(v));
  if (multi || key === 'yilMulti') st.sl[key] = has ? cur.filter(x =&gt; X.keyOf(x) !== X.keyOf(v)) : cur.concat([v]);
  else st.sl[key] = has &amp;&amp; cur.length === 1 ? [] : [v];
}

function api(dash, st, ms, tile) {
  const keyEq = (a, b) =&gt; a.t === b.t &amp;&amp; a.c === b.c;
  const yearSl = () =&gt; { const k = (dash.slicers || []).find(k =&gt; SL[k] &amp;&amp; SL[k].t === 'Tarih' &amp;&amp; SL[k].c === 'Yil'); return k ? (st.sl[k] || []) : []; };
  function spec(o) {
    o = o || {}; const ex = (o.except || []).map(e =&gt; ({ t: e[0], c: e[1] })); const isEx = s =&gt; ex.some(e =&gt; keyEq(e, s));
    let f = [];
    (dash.slicers || []).forEach(k =&gt; { const d = SL[k]; if (!d || d.rls) return; const v = st.sl[k]; if (!v || !v.length) return; const s = { t: d.t, c: d.c, values: v.slice() };
      if (isEx(s) &amp;&amp; (s.t === 'Tarih' || MODEL.tables[s.t].kind === 'param')) return; f.push(s); });
    if (!o.noRls &amp;&amp; st.sl.viewAs &amp;&amp; st.sl.viewAs.length) f.push({ t: 'Calisanlar', c: 'AdSoyad', values: st.sl.viewAs.slice() });
    if (st.cross &amp;&amp; !isEx(st.cross)) f.push({ t: st.cross.t, c: st.cross.c, values: [st.cross.v] });
    (o.add || []).forEach(s =&gt; { f = f.filter(x =&gt; !keyEq(x, s)); f.push(s); });
    return f;
  }
  const msx = o =&gt; { if (!o || !o.extraMeasures) return ms; const m2 = Object.assign({}, ms); Object.keys(o.extraMeasures).forEach(k =&gt; { m2[k] = { name: k, expr: o.extraMeasures[k] }; }); return m2; };
  const Q = (rows, values, o) =&gt; { o = o || {}; const sp = spec(o); return X.query({ rows: rows.map(pc), measures: msx(o), values, slicers: sp, totals: o.totals !== false, keepBlank: !!o.keepBlank }); };
  const M = (expr, o) =&gt; { const sp = spec(o); return X.evalMeasure(msx(o), expr, sp, { shadow: sp }); };
  const ys = yearSl();
  const dr = tile &amp;&amp; tile.drill ? (st.drill[tile.id] || { lv: 0, path: [] }) : null;
  const a = {
    Q, M, spec, fmt: V.fmt,
    yearLabel: ys.length === 1 ? String(ys[0]) : ys.length ? ys.slice().sort().join(', ') : 'Tüm yıllar',
    timeAxis: ys.length === 1 ? 'Tarih[Ay]' : 'Tarih[YilAy]',
    P: { fiyat: st.sl.fiyat &amp;&amp; st.sl.fiyat.length ? st.sl.fiyat[0] : 0, para: (st.sl.para &amp;&amp; st.sl.para[0]) || 'TRY', viewAs: (st.sl.viewAs &amp;&amp; st.sl.viewAs[0]) || null, topn: (st.sl.topn &amp;&amp; st.sl.topn[0]) || 5 },
    sel: (cats, t, c) =&gt; st.cross &amp;&amp; st.cross.t === t &amp;&amp; st.cross.c === c ? cats.findIndex(k =&gt; X.keyOf(k) === X.keyOf(st.cross.v)) : null,
    slicerVals: k =&gt; st.sl[k] || [],
    level: dr ? dr.lv : 0,
    dcol: dr ? tile.drill[dr.lv] : null,
    dspec: dr ? dr.path.map((v, i) =&gt; Object.assign(pc(tile.drill[i]), { values: [v] })) : [],
    tipx: (col, cats) =&gt; i =&gt; {
      const c = pc(col); const add = [{ t: c.t, c: c.c, values: [cats[i]] }];
      const r = Q(['Tarih[YilAy]'], ['Net Satış'], { add, totals: false, except: [['Tarih', 'Yil']] });
      const yoy = M('[YoY %]', { add });
      return { title: 'Araç ipucu sayfası · ' + cats[i], cards: [['Net Satış', V.fmt(M('[Net Satış]', { add }), 'para', true)], ['Geçen yıla göre', yoy == null ? '—' : pct(yoy)], ['Kâr marjı', V.fmt(M('[Kâr Marjı %]', { add }), 'yuzde')]], spark: r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[0]).slice(-12), sparkLabel: 'Son 12 ay net satış' };
    }
  };
  return a;
}
const WIDE = ['column', 'bar', 'line', 'donut', 'waterfall', 'funnel', 'treemap', 'scatter', 'map', 'decomp', 'bullet'];
function wrapV(w) { const o = {}; Object.keys(V).forEach(k =&gt; { o[k] = WIDE.includes(k) ? (d, opt) =&gt; V[k](d, Object.assign({ W: w }, opt || {})) : V[k]; }); return o; }
function renderTile(dash, tile, st, ms, w) {
  const a = api(dash, st, ms, tile); a.V = wrapV(w);
  try { const r = tile.render(a); return typeof r === 'string' || r == null ? { html: r || '', cats: null } : r; }
  catch (e) { if (!e.dax &amp;&amp; W.console) console.error(tile.id, e); return { html: '&lt;div class="msg err"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Hesaplama hatası&lt;/b&gt;' + V.esc(e.message) + '&lt;/div&gt;', cats: null, err: e }; }
}
/* drill işlemleri */
function drill(st, tile, op, v) {
  const d = st.drill[tile.id] || (st.drill[tile.id] = { lv: 0, path: [], mode: false }); const n = tile.drill.length;
  if (op === 'up' &amp;&amp; d.lv &gt; 0) { d.lv--; d.path = d.path.slice(0, d.lv); }
  if (op === 'mode') d.mode = !d.mode;
  if (op === 'next' &amp;&amp; d.lv &lt; n - 1) { d.lv++; d.path = d.path.slice(0, d.lv - 1).concat(d.path.length &gt;= d.lv ? [d.path[d.lv - 1]] : []); if (d.path.length &lt; d.lv) d.path = d.path.slice(0, d.lv - 1); d.path = d.path.filter(x =&gt; x !== undefined); d.all = true; }
  if (op === 'into' &amp;&amp; d.lv &lt; n - 1) { d.path = d.path.slice(0, d.lv).concat([v]); d.lv++; }
  // tüm düzeyi genişletirken üst düzeyin filtresi yok: path, lv'den kısa olabilir
  return d;
}
W.PBI_DASHCORE = { SL, initState, toggleSlicer, api, renderTile, drill, pc, wrapV };
})(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : globalThis);

  if (op === 'reset') { d.lv = 0; d.path = []; d.mode = false; }&lt;/parameter&gt;

[d.path[d.lv - 1]] : []); if (d.path.length &lt; d.lv) d.path = d.path.slice(0, d.lv - 1); d.path = d.path.filter(x =&gt; x !== undefined); d.all = true; }&lt;/parameter&gt;

&lt;tool name="Write"&gt;
&lt;parameter name="content"&gt;// Dashboard smoke test: her dashboardın her görselini varsayılan durumda ve bazı etkileşimlerle çiz
global.window = global; const D = '/home/claude/pbi/';
['data', 'dax', 'measures', 'patterns', 'viz', 'dashboards', 'dashcore'].forEach(f =&gt; require(D + f + '.js'));
const X = PBI_DAX, C = PBI_DASHCORE;
const ms = {}; PBI_MEASURES.forEach(m =&gt; ms[m.name] = { name: m.name, expr: m.expr });
let bad = 0;
const missing = new Set();
PBI_DASHES.forEach(d =&gt; {
  d.tiles.forEach(t =&gt; (t.measures || []).forEach(n =&gt; { if (!ms[n]) missing.add(d.id + ':' + t.id + ' -&gt; ' + n); }));
  (d.build || []).forEach(b =&gt; (b[2] || []).forEach(n =&gt; { if (!ms[n]) missing.add(d.id + ':build -&gt; ' + n); }));
  const variants = [['default', st =&gt; st]];
  if (d.slicers.includes('kategori')) variants.push(['kat', st =&gt; { st.sl.kategori = ['Elektronik']; return st; }]);
  if (d.slicers.includes('viewAs')) variants.push(['rls', st =&gt; { st.sl.viewAs = [C.SL.viewAs.vals[0]]; return st; }]);
  if (d.slicers.includes('para')) variants.push(['usd', st =&gt; { st.sl.para = ['USD']; st.sl.fiyat = [0.1]; return st; }]);
  if (d.slicers.includes('yil')) variants.push(['allyears', st =&gt; { st.sl.yil = []; return st; }]);
  variants.push(['cross', st =&gt; { st.cross = { t: 'Satislar', c: 'Kanal', v: 'Web' }; return st; }]);
  variants.forEach(([vn, fn]) =&gt; {
    const st = fn(C.initState(d)); const t0 = Date.now();
    X.batch(() =&gt; d.tiles.forEach(t =&gt; {
      const t1 = Date.now(); const r = C.renderTile(d, t, st, ms, 500); const dt = Date.now() - t1;
      if (r.err || /msg err/.test(r.html)) { bad++; console.log('ERR', d.id, vn, t.id, r.err ? r.err.message : r.html.slice(0, 200)); }
      if (dt &gt; 400) console.log('slow', d.id, vn, t.id, dt + 'ms');
      if (t.drill) { const st2 = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(st)); const cats = r.cats || []; if (cats.length) { C.drill(st2, t, 'into', cats[0]); const r2 = C.renderTile(d, t, st2, ms, 500); if (r2.err) { bad++; console.log('ERR drill', d.id, t.id, r2.err.message); } } }
    }));
    console.log(d.id.padEnd(10), vn.padEnd(9), (Date.now() - t0) + 'ms');
  });
});
console.log('missing measures:', [...missing]);
console.log('tiles', PBI_DASHES.reduce((a, d) =&gt; a + d.tiles.length, 0), 'bad', bad);

function hl(src) {
  const re = /(\/\/[^\n]*|--[^\n]*|\/\*[\s\S]*?(?:\*\/|$))|("(?:[^"]|"")*"?)|('[^'\n]*'?)(\[[^\]\n]*\]?)?|([A-Za-z_À-￿][\w.À-￿]*)(\[[^\]\n]*\]?)?|(\[[^\]\n]*\]?)|(\b\d+(?:\.\d+)?\b)|([\s\S])/g;
  let out = '', m;
  while ((m = re.exec(src))) {
    if (m[1]) out += '&lt;span class="c"&gt;' + esc(m[1]) + '&lt;/span&gt;';
    else if (m[2]) out += '&lt;span class="s"&gt;' + esc(m[2]) + '&lt;/span&gt;';
    else if (m[3]) out += '&lt;span class="v"&gt;' + esc(m[3]) + (m[4] ? esc(m[4]) : '') + '&lt;/span&gt;';
    else if (m[5]) { const w = m[5], up = w.toUpperCase(); if (m[6]) out += '&lt;span class="v"&gt;' + esc(w + m[6]) + '&lt;/span&gt;'; else if (KWS.has(up) &amp;&amp; !/^\s*\(/.test(src.slice(re.lastIndex))) out += '&lt;span class="k"&gt;' + esc(w) + '&lt;/span&gt;'; else if (FNSET.has(up) &amp;&amp; /^\s*\(/.test(src.slice(re.lastIndex))) out += '&lt;span class="f"&gt;' + esc(w) + '&lt;/span&gt;'; else if (MODEL.tables[w]) out += '&lt;span class="v"&gt;' + esc(w) + '&lt;/span&gt;'; else out += esc(w); }
    else if (m[7]) out += '&lt;span class="ms"&gt;' + esc(m[7]) + '&lt;/span&gt;';
    else if (m[8]) out += '&lt;span class="n"&gt;' + m[8] + '&lt;/span&gt;';
    else out += esc(m[9]);
  }
  return out;
}

function guide(v) {
  const g = GUIDES[v]; if (!g) return ''; const open = !(P.gclosed || {})[v];
  return '&lt;details class="guide" data-guide="' + v + '"' + (open ? ' open' : '') + '&gt;&lt;summary&gt;&lt;span class="gi" aria-hidden="true"&gt;i&lt;/span&gt;' + esc(g[0]) + '&lt;/summary&gt;&lt;div class="gbody"&gt;&lt;p&gt;' + g[1] + '&lt;/p&gt;' + (g[2] ? '&lt;ul&gt;' + g[2].map(x =&gt; '&lt;li&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + x[0] + '&lt;/b&gt; ' + x[1] + '&lt;/li&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/ul&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/details&gt;';
}
const head = (eyebrow, title, desc, v) =&gt; '&lt;div class="phead"&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;' + eyebrow + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h1&gt;' + title + '&lt;/h1&gt;' + (desc ? '&lt;p class="muted"&gt;' + desc + '&lt;/p&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/div&gt;' + guide(v);

/* ---------- İlerleme ---------- */
const exKey = (l, i) =&gt; l.id + ':' + i;
function lessonState(l) { if (P.done[l.id]) return 'done'; const n = (l.ex || []).filter((e, i) =&gt; P.ex[exKey(l, i)]).length; return n ? 'part' : 'none'; }
function pct(ids) { if (!ids.length) return 0; return Math.round(ids.filter(id =&gt; P.done[id]).length / ids.length * 100); }
const levelIds = lv =&gt; LESSONS.filter(l =&gt; l.lvl === lv).map(l =&gt; l.id);
function myLevel() { let best = null; for (const L of LEVELS) { const ids = levelIds(L.id); if (ids.length &amp;&amp; pct(ids) &gt;= 80) best = L.id; else break; } if (P.placement &amp;&amp; (!best || LVI(P.placement) &gt; LVI(best))) return P.placement; return best; }
function nextLesson(ctxPath) { const list = ctxPath &amp;&amp; PMAP[ctxPath] ? PMAP[ctxPath].lessons.map(id =&gt; LMAP[id]).filter(Boolean) : LESSONS; return list.find(l =&gt; !P.done[l.id]) || null; }
function checkLessonDone(l) { const ex = l.ex || []; if (ex.length &amp;&amp; ex.every((e, i) =&gt; P.ex[exKey(l, i)]) &amp;&amp; !P.done[l.id]) { P.done[l.id] = true; addXP(10); save(); toast('Ders tamamlandı: ' + l.title + ' (+10 XP)'); return true; } return false; }

/* ---------- Kabuk ---------- */
const VIEWS = {};
function view(name, def) { VIEWS[name] = def; }
let S = { view: 'home', id: null, step: 0, ctx: null };
const parentOf = v =&gt; ({ lesson: 'curr', path: 'paths' })[v] || v;
function renderNav() {
  const cur = parentOf(S.view);
  $('#nav').innerHTML = NAV.map(([g, items]) =&gt; '&lt;span class="navgrp"&gt;' + g + '&lt;/span&gt;' + items.map(([v, t, i]) =&gt; '&lt;button data-go="' + v + '" aria-current="' + (cur === v) + '"&gt;' + ic(i) + t + '&lt;/button&gt;').join('')).join('');
  const inTab = TABNAV.some(n =&gt; n[0] === cur);
  $('#tabbar').innerHTML = TABNAV.map(([v, t, i]) =&gt; '&lt;button data-go="' + v + '" aria-current="' + (cur === v || (v === 'more' &amp;&amp; !inTab)) + '"&gt;' + ic(i) + '&lt;span&gt;' + t + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/button&gt;').join('');
}
function renderStats() {
  const st = streak(), done = Object.keys(P.done).filter(k =&gt; LMAP[k]).length; const lv = myLevel();
  const ss = $('#sideStats'); if (ss) ss.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="stat"&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + P.xp + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;span&gt;XP&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="stat"&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + st + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;span&gt;günlük seri&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="stat"&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + done + '/' + LESSONS.length + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;span&gt;ders&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="stat"&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + (lv || '—') + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;span&gt;' + (lv ? lvName(lv) : 'seviye') + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;a class="sibling" href="' + SQL_URL + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;SQLPatika\'ya geç ↗&lt;/a&gt;';
  const ms = $('#miniStats'); if (ms) ms.innerHTML = '&lt;b&gt;' + P.xp + '&lt;/b&gt;&amp;nbsp;XP · &lt;b&gt;' + st + '&lt;/b&gt;&amp;nbsp;gün';
}
let lastHash = location.hash;
function hashFor(v, id) { const d = VIEWS[v]; if (!d || v === 'home') return ''; return (d.hash || v) + (id ? '-' + id : ''); }
function go(v, id, opts) {
  opts = opts || {};
  if (v === 'path' &amp;&amp; !id) v = 'paths';
  S = { view: v, id: id || null, step: opts.step || 0, ctx: opts.ctx !== undefined ? opts.ctx : (v === 'lesson' ? S.ctx : null) };
  if (v === 'path') S.ctx = id;
  const hash = hashFor(v, id);
  try { history.replaceState(null, '', hash ? ('#' + hash) : location.pathname + location.search); lastHash = location.hash; } catch (e) {}
  render(); if (!opts.keepScroll) window.scrollTo(0, 0); const m = $('#main'); if (m) m.focus({ preventScroll: true });
}
function render() {
  renderNav(); renderStats();
  const d = VIEWS[S.view] || VIEWS.home, main = $('#main');
  main.innerHTML = d.render(S.id);
  enhance(main);
  if (d.mount) d.mount(S.id);
}
function enhance(root) {
  root.querySelectorAll('pre.dax').forEach(pre =&gt; {
    if (pre.dataset.done) return; pre.dataset.done = 1; const src = pre.textContent; pre.innerHTML = hl(src);
    const wrap = document.createElement('div'); wrap.className = 'codebox'; pre.parentNode.insertBefore(wrap, pre); wrap.appendChild(pre);
    if (!pre.hasAttribute('data-notry')) { const b = document.createElement('button'); b.className = 'try'; b.type = 'button'; b.textContent = 'Stüdyoda dene'; b.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; openInStudio(src)); wrap.appendChild(b); }
  });
  root.querySelectorAll('pre.m').forEach(pre =&gt; { if (pre.dataset.done) return; pre.dataset.done = 1; pre.innerHTML = hlM(pre.textContent); });
  root.querySelectorAll('.theory table').forEach(t =&gt; { if (t.parentNode.classList.contains('tbl-wrap')) return; const w = document.createElement('div'); w.className = 'tbl-wrap'; t.parentNode.insertBefore(w, t); w.appendChild(t); });
}
function openInStudio(src) {
  src = src.trim();
  const isQ = /^\s*(DEFINE|EVALUATE)\b/i.test(src) || /\bEVALUATE\b/.test(src);
  if (!isQ) {
    const defs = splitDefs(src);
    if (defs.length &gt; 1) { defs.slice(0, -1).forEach(d =&gt; { if (d.name &amp;&amp; !LIBMAP[d.name]) upsertMine(d.name, d.expr, true); }); src = defs[defs.length - 1].name ? defs[defs.length - 1].name + ' = ' + defs[defs.length - 1].expr : defs[defs.length - 1].expr; }
  }
  ST.mode = isQ ? 'q' : 'm'; ST.code[ST.mode] = src; saveStudio(); go('studio');
}
function splitDefs(src) {
  const lines = src.split('\n'); const out = []; let cur = null;
  lines.forEach(line =&gt; { const m = line.match(/^([^\s\-\/=()\[\]"'{},][^=()\[\]"{},]*?)\s*:?=(?!=)\s*(.*)$/); if (m &amp;&amp; !/^(VAR|RETURN)\b/i.test(m[1].trim())) { cur = { name: m[1].trim(), expr: m[2] }; out.push(cur); } else if (cur) cur.expr += '\n' + line; else { cur = { name: null, expr: line }; out.push(cur); } });
  return out.map(d =&gt; ({ name: d.name, expr: d.expr.replace(/^\s*\n/, '').replace(/\s+$/, '') })).filter(d =&gt; d.expr.trim() &amp;&amp; !/^--/.test(d.expr.trim()));
}
/* ölçü + bağımlılıklarının DAX bloğu */
function daxDeps(names) { const uniq = [], seen = new Set(); const add = n =&gt; { if (seen.has(n)) return; seen.add(n); const m = LIBMAP[n]; if (!m) return; const re = /(\w|')?\[([^\]]+)\]/g; let mm; while ((mm = re.exec(m.expr))) { if (!mm[1] &amp;&amp; LIBMAP[mm[2]]) add(mm[2]); } uniq.push(n); }; (names || []).forEach(add); return uniq; }
function daxBlock(names, title, open) {
  const uniq = daxDeps(names); if (!uniq.length) return '';
  const code = uniq.map(n =&gt; n + ' =\n' + LIBMAP[n].expr).join('\n\n');
  return '&lt;details class="daxshow"' + (open ? ' open' : '') + '&gt;&lt;summary&gt;' + esc(title || 'DAX ölçüleri') + ' (' + uniq.length + ')&lt;/summary&gt;&lt;pre class="dax" data-notry&gt;' + esc(code) + '&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;button class="btn sm" data-try="' + esc(uniq.map(n =&gt; n + ' = ' + LIBMAP[n].expr).join('\n')) + '"&gt;Stüdyoda aç&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/details&gt;';
}

/* ---------- Genişlik yardımcıları: SVG viewBox'u kutunun gerçek genişliğine eşitle ---------- */
function mainW() { const m = document.getElementById('main'); const w = (m ? m.clientWidth : window.innerWidth) - 32; return Math.min(1180, Math.max(300, w)); }
function spanW(span) { const ww = window.innerWidth; const eff = ww &lt;= 760 ? 12 : ww &lt;= 1080 ? (span &lt;= 3 ? 6 : 12) : span; const inner = mainW() - 34; return Math.max(260, Math.round((inner - (12 / eff - 1) * 12) * eff / 12) - 34); }
function studioW() { const ww = window.innerWidth; const side = ww &gt; 980 ? 256 : 0; return Math.max(280, Math.min(900, mainW() - side - 36)); }
let tipsBound = false;
function bindMarks() { if (!tipsBound) { V.bindTips(document.body); tipsBound = true; } }

/* ---------- Ana sayfa ---------- */
function lessonRow(l, ctx) {
  const st = lessonState(l), n = (l.ex || []).length; const nd = (l.ex || []).filter(e =&gt; e.t !== 'mc').length;
  return '&lt;button class="litem" data-lesson="' + l.id + '"' + (ctx ? ' data-ctx="' + ctx + '"' : '') + '&gt;&lt;span class="check ' + (st === 'done' ? 'done' : st === 'part' ? 'part' : '') + '"&gt;' + (st === 'done' ? ic('tick', 3.2) : '') + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span style="min-width:0"&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + esc(l.title) + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;small&gt;' + esc(l.sum || '') + '&lt;/small&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class="row" style="gap:6px;flex-wrap:nowrap"&gt;' + (nd ? '&lt;span class="chip dx"&gt;' + nd + ' DAX&lt;/span&gt;' : '') + '&lt;span class="chip"&gt;' + (n ? n + ' alıştırma' : 'okuma') + '&lt;/span&gt;' + lvBadge(l.lvl) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/button&gt;';
}
function counts() {
  const ex = LESSONS.reduce((a, l) =&gt; a + (l.ex || []).length, 0), dx = LESSONS.reduce((a, l) =&gt; a + (l.ex || []).filter(e =&gt; e.t !== 'mc').length, 0);
  const cmds = (window.PBI_MENUS || []).reduce((a, g) =&gt; a + g.tabs.reduce((b, t) =&gt; b + t.cmds.length, 0), 0);
  return { ex, dx, cmds, pat: (window.PBI_PATTERNS || []).length, dash: (window.PBI_DASHES || []).length, tricks: (window.PBI_TRICKS || []).length };
}

function pathCard(p) {
  const ids = p.lessons, pc = pct(ids);
  return '&lt;button class="pcard" data-go="path" data-id="' + p.id + '"&gt;&lt;span class="row" style="justify-content:space-between"&gt;&lt;span class="ic" style="background:' + p.color + '"&gt;' + p.short + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class="chip"&gt;' + ids.length + ' ders&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h3&gt;' + esc(p.name) + '&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;p&gt;' + esc(p.desc) + '&lt;/p&gt;&lt;span class="bar"&gt;&lt;i style="width:' + pc + '%;background:' + p.color + '"&gt;&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span class="foot"&gt;&lt;span&gt;%' + pc + ' tamamlandı&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;~' + Math.max(1, Math.round(ids.reduce((a, id) =&gt; a + (LMAP[id].min || 10), 0) / 60)) + ' saat&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/button&gt;';
}
function viewPaths() { return '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;' + head('Patikalar', 'Hangi hedef için öğreniyorsun?', 'Her patika, dersleri o hedefe göre seçer ve sıralar.', 'paths') + '&lt;div class="paths"&gt;' + PATHS.map(pathCard).join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;'; }
function viewPath(id) {
  const p = PMAP[id]; if (!p) return viewPaths();
  const ls = p.lessons.map(x =&gt; LMAP[x]), nx = ls.find(l =&gt; !P.done[l.id]);
  let h = '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;&lt;div class="crumbs"&gt;&lt;button data-go="paths"&gt;Patikalar&lt;/button&gt;&lt;span&gt;/&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;' + esc(p.name) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;section class="panel hero-main"&gt;&lt;div class="row"&gt;&lt;span class="ic" style="background:' + p.color + ';height:46px;min-width:46px"&gt;' + p.short + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;div class="grow"&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Patika&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h1 style="font-size:1.9rem"&gt;' + esc(p.name) + '&lt;/h1&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p&gt;' + esc(p.long || p.desc) + '&lt;/p&gt;';
  if (p.skills) h += '&lt;div class="row" style="gap:6px"&gt;' + p.skills.map(s =&gt; '&lt;span class="chip"&gt;' + esc(s) + '&lt;/span&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;div class="bar"&gt;&lt;i style="width:' + pct(p.lessons) + '%;background:' + p.color + '"&gt;&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="row"&gt;' + (nx ? '&lt;button class="btn pri" data-lesson="' + nx.id + '" data-ctx="' + p.id + '"&gt;' + (pct(p.lessons) ? 'Devam et' : 'Patikaya başla') + '&lt;/button&gt;' : '&lt;span class="chip"&gt;Patika tamamlandı&lt;/span&gt;&lt;button class="btn pri" data-go="final"&gt;Final projesi&lt;/button&gt;') + '&lt;span class="muted num"&gt;' + ls.filter(l =&gt; P.done[l.id]).length + '/' + ls.length + ' ders · %' + pct(p.lessons) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/section&gt;';
  LEVELS.forEach(L =&gt; { const items = ls.filter(l =&gt; l.lvl === L.id); if (!items.length) return; h += '&lt;section class="lvl-block"&gt;&lt;div class="lvl-head"&gt;' + lvBadge(L.id) + '&lt;h2&gt;' + L.name + '&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;span class="muted" style="font-size:.88rem"&gt;' + items.length + ' ders&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="panel lessons"&gt;' + items.map(l =&gt; lessonRow(l, p.id)).join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/section&gt;'; });
  return h + '&lt;/div&gt;';
}
function viewMore() {
  return '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;&lt;div class="phead"&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Menü&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h1&gt;Tüm bölümler&lt;/h1&gt;&lt;/div&gt;' + NAV.map(([g, items]) =&gt; '&lt;section class="lvl-block"&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;' + g + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;div class="tools"&gt;' + items.map(([v, t, i]) =&gt; '&lt;button class="tool" data-go="' + v + '"&gt;&lt;span class="tic"&gt;' + ic(i) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + t + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;small&gt;' + esc((GUIDES[v] || ['', ''])[0]) + '&lt;/small&gt;&lt;/button&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/section&gt;').join('') + '&lt;a class="tool" href="' + SQL_URL + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener"&gt;&lt;span class="tic"&gt;' + ic('term') + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;b&gt;SQLPatika ↗&lt;/b&gt;&lt;small&gt;Aynı veriyle SQL öğren&lt;/small&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';

/* ---------- Müfredat ---------- */
const currFilter = { lv: 'all', q: '' };
function viewCurr() {
  return '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;' + head('Tüm dersler', 'Müfredat: A1 → C2', LESSONS.length + ' ders, altı seviye. İstediğin dersi açabilirsin; sıra öneridir.', 'curr') + '&lt;div class="row"&gt;&lt;input class="input" id="currQ" placeholder="Ders ara: CALCULATE, RLS, Power Query, araç ipucu…" value="' + esc(currFilter.q) + '" style="max-width:360px" aria-label="Ders ara"&gt;&lt;div class="filters"&gt;' + ['all'].concat(LEVELS.map(l =&gt; l.id)).map(x =&gt; '&lt;button data-lvf="' + x + '" aria-pressed="' + (currFilter.lv === x) + '"&gt;' + (x === 'all' ? 'Tümü' : x + ' · ' + lvName(x)) + '&lt;/button&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id="currList"&gt;' + currList() + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
}
function currList() {
  const q = currFilter.q.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr'); let h = '';
  LEVELS.forEach(L =&gt; {
    if (currFilter.lv !== 'all' &amp;&amp; currFilter.lv !== L.id) return;
    const ls = LESSONS.filter(l =&gt; l.lvl === L.id &amp;&amp; (!q || (l.title + ' ' + (l.sum || '') + ' ' + l.mod + ' ' + l.body).toLocaleLowerCase('tr').includes(q)));
    if (!ls.length) return;
    const mods = []; ls.forEach(l =&gt; { let m = mods.find(x =&gt; x.n === l.mod); if (!m) { m = { n: l.mod, items: [] }; mods.push(m); } m.items.push(l); });
    const ids = levelIds(L.id);
    h += '&lt;section class="lvl-block" style="margin-bottom:28px"&gt;&lt;div class="lvl-head"&gt;' + lvBadge(L.id) + '&lt;h2&gt;' + L.name + '&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;span class="muted" style="font-size:.88rem"&gt;' + esc(L.desc) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
    h += '&lt;div class="panel cando"&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Bu seviyenin sonunda…&lt;/span&gt;&lt;ul&gt;' + L.can.map(c =&gt; '&lt;li&gt;' + esc(c) + '&lt;/li&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="cando-side"&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;DAX uzunluğu&lt;/span&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + esc(L.dax) + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;span class="bar"&gt;&lt;i style="width:' + pct(ids) + '%"&gt;&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;small class="muted"&gt;' + ids.filter(i =&gt; P.done[i]).length + '/' + ids.length + ' ders tamamlandı&lt;/small&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
    h += '&lt;div class="panel lessons"&gt;' + mods.map(m =&gt; '&lt;div class="mod-head"&gt;' + esc(m.n) + '&lt;/div&gt;' + m.items.map(l =&gt; lessonRow(l)).join('')).join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/section&gt;';
  });
  return h || '&lt;div class="panel empty"&gt;Aramana uyan ders bulunamadı.&lt;/div&gt;';
}

/* ---------- Ders ---------- */
let ED = null;
function viewLesson() {
  const l = LMAP[S.id]; if (!l) return '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;&lt;div class="panel empty"&gt;Ders bulunamadı.&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  P.last = l.id; save(); const ex = l.ex || [];
  let h = '&lt;div class="wrap" style="gap:18px"&gt;&lt;div class="lhead"&gt;&lt;div class="crumbs"&gt;' + (S.ctx &amp;&amp; PMAP[S.ctx] ? '&lt;button data-go="path" data-id="' + S.ctx + '"&gt;' + esc(PMAP[S.ctx].name) + '&lt;/button&gt;' : '&lt;button data-go="curr"&gt;Dersler&lt;/button&gt;') + '&lt;span&gt;/&lt;/span&gt;' + lvBadge(l.lvl) + '&lt;span&gt;' + esc(l.mod) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;div class="row" style="justify-content:space-between;align-items:end"&gt;&lt;h1 style="font-size:clamp(1.5rem,3.4vw,2.1rem)"&gt;' + esc(l.title) + '&lt;/h1&gt;&lt;span class="muted" style="font-size:.85rem"&gt;~' + (l.min || 10) + ' dk&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;div class="steps"&gt;&lt;button data-step="0" aria-current="' + (S.step === 0) + '" class="' + (P.seen[l.id] ? 'done' : '') + '"&gt;Konu&lt;/button&gt;' + ex.map((e, i) =&gt; '&lt;button data-step="' + (i + 1) + '" aria-current="' + (S.step === i + 1) + '" class="' + (P.ex[exKey(l, i)] ? 'done' : '') + '" aria-label="Alıştırma ' + (i + 1) + '"&gt;' + (i + 1) + (e.t !== 'mc' ? '&lt;i class="dxdot" title="DAX"&gt;&lt;/i&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/button&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  if (S.step === 0) {
    h += '&lt;article class="panel theory"&gt;' + l.body + '&lt;/article&gt;&lt;div class="pager"&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;/span&gt;' + (ex.length ? '&lt;button class="btn pri" data-step="1"&gt;Alıştırmalara geç (' + ex.length + ')&lt;/button&gt;' : (P.done[l.id] ? nextBtn(l) : '&lt;button class="btn pri" data-act="read-done"&gt;Okudum, dersi tamamla&lt;/button&gt;')) + '&lt;/div&gt;';
  } else if (S.step &lt;= ex.length) {
    const e = ex[S.step - 1], k = exKey(l, S.step - 1);
    h += e.t === 'mc' ? mcView(e, k) : daxView(e, k);
    h += '&lt;div class="pager"&gt;&lt;button class="btn" data-step="' + (S.step - 1) + '"&gt;Geri&lt;/button&gt;' + (S.step &lt; ex.length ? '&lt;button class="btn" data-step="' + (S.step + 1) + '"&gt;Sonraki alıştırma&lt;/button&gt;' : (P.done[l.id] ? nextBtn(l) : '&lt;button class="btn" data-step="0"&gt;Konuya dön&lt;/button&gt;')) + '&lt;/div&gt;';
  }
  return h + '&lt;/div&gt;';
}
function nextBtn(l) {
  let nx = null;
  if (S.ctx &amp;&amp; PMAP[S.ctx]) { const arr = PMAP[S.ctx].lessons; const i = arr.indexOf(l.id); nx = LMAP[arr.slice(i + 1).find(id =&gt; !P.done[id]) || arr[i + 1]]; }
  else nx = LESSONS.slice(l._i + 1).find(x =&gt; !P.done[x.id]) || LESSONS[l._i + 1];
  return nx ? '&lt;button class="btn pri" data-lesson="' + nx.id + '"&gt;Sonraki ders: ' + esc(nx.title) + '&lt;/button&gt;' : '&lt;button class="btn pri" data-go="final"&gt;Final projesine geç&lt;/button&gt;';
}
function mcView(e, k) {
  const solved = P.ex[k];
  let h = '&lt;div class="panel brief" style="max-width:820px"&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Soru' + (solved ? ' · çözüldü' : '') + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;div class="q"&gt;' + e.q + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="mc' + (e.thumbs ? ' mc-vis' : '') + '" id="mc"&gt;';
  e.o.forEach((o, i) =&gt; { h += '&lt;button data-mc="' + i + '" class="' + (solved &amp;&amp; i === e.a ? 'right' : '') + '"&gt;' + (e.thumbs ? V.thumb(e.thumbs[i]) : '&lt;span class="key"&gt;' + String.fromCharCode(65 + i) + '&lt;/span&gt;') + '&lt;span&gt;' + o + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/button&gt;'; });
  return h + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id="mcFb"&gt;' + (solved &amp;&amp; e.why ? '&lt;div class="msg ok"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Doğru.&lt;/b&gt;' + e.why + '&lt;/div&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
}
const DEFAULT_GRID = [{ t: 'Kategoriler', c: 'Kategori' }, { t: 'Tarih', c: 'Yil' }];
function daxView(e, k) {
  const solved = P.ex[k]; const isQ = e.t === 'query';
  let h = '&lt;div class="ex-grid"&gt;&lt;div class="panel brief"&gt;&lt;div class="row" style="justify-content:space-between"&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;' + (isQ ? 'DAX sorgusu' : 'Ölçü yaz') + (solved ? ' · çözüldü' : '') + '&lt;/span&gt;' + (!isQ ? '&lt;span class="chip dx"&gt;' + esc(e.name) + '&lt;/span&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="q"&gt;' + e.q + '&lt;/div&gt;';
  if (!isQ) { const g = e.grid || DEFAULT_GRID; h += '&lt;div class="note" style="margin:0"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Nasıl kontrol edilir?&lt;/b&gt;Ölçün ' + (e.noTotal ? '' : 'toplamda ve ') + g.map(x =&gt; '&lt;code&gt;' + colKey(x) + '&lt;/code&gt;').join(' ile ') + ' kırılımlarında hesaplanır ve doğru çözümle karşılaştırılır. Hazır ölçüler (ör. &lt;code&gt;[Net Satış]&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code&gt;[Hedef]&lt;/code&gt;) kullanılabilir.&lt;/div&gt;'; }
  h += '&lt;div class="row" style="gap:8px"&gt;' + (e.hint ? '&lt;button class="btn sm" data-act="hint"&gt;İpucu&lt;/button&gt;' : '') + '&lt;button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="sol"&gt;Çözümü göster&lt;/button&gt;&lt;button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="ex-studio"&gt;Stüdyoda aç&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id="hintBox"&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id="solBox"&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;div class="work"&gt;&lt;div class="ed-shell"&gt;&lt;div class="ed-top"&gt;&lt;span&gt;' + (isQ ? 'EVALUATE sorgusu' : esc(e.name) + ' =') + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;Ctrl/⌘ + Enter: kontrol et&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id="ed"&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="ed-actions"&gt;&lt;button class="btn pri" data-act="check"&gt;Kontrol et&lt;/button&gt;&lt;button class="btn" data-act="run"&gt;Yalnızca çalıştır&lt;/button&gt;&lt;button class="btn ghost sm" data-act="reset-ed"&gt;Baştan başla&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id="out"&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  return h;
}
function mountLesson() {
  const l = LMAP[S.id]; if (!l) return;
  if (S.step === 0) { if (!P.seen[l.id]) { P.seen[l.id] = true; save(); } return; }
  const e = (l.ex || [])[S.step - 1]; if (!e || e.t === 'mc') return;
  const k = exKey(l, S.step - 1); const init = P.drafts[k] != null ? P.drafts[k] : (e.start || (e.t === 'query' ? 'EVALUATE\n' : ''));
  ED = makeEditor($('#ed'), init, () =&gt; checkEx(true), e.t === 'query');
  ED.onChange = v =&gt; { P.drafts[k] = v; save(); };
  if (P.ex[k] &amp;&amp; P.drafts[k] != null) checkEx(false, true);
}
function cmpVal(a, b) {
  const bl = v =&gt; v === null || v === undefined || (typeof v === 'number' &amp;&amp; isNaN(v));
  if (bl(a) &amp;&amp; bl(b)) return true; if (bl(a) || bl(b)) return false;
  if (typeof a === 'number' &amp;&amp; typeof b === 'number') return Math.abs(a - b) &lt;= 1e-6 * Math.max(1, Math.abs(b));
  if (a instanceof Date &amp;&amp; b instanceof Date) return a.getTime() === b.getTime();
  return String(a) === String(b);
}
function gridEval(ms, expr, grid, noTotal) {
  const out = [];
  if (!noTotal) out.push({ g: 'Toplam', rows: [{ k: 'Toplam', v: X.evalMeasure(ms, expr, []) }] });
  grid.forEach(col =&gt; { const r = X.query({ rows: [col], measures: ms, values: [expr], slicers: [], totals: false, keepBlank: true }); out.push({ g: colKey(col), rows: r.rows.map(x =&gt; ({ k: x.keys[0], v: x.vals[0] })) }); });
  return out;
}
const explainBox = e =&gt; (e.explain ? '&lt;div class="explain"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Mantığı&lt;/b&gt;' + e.explain + '&lt;/div&gt;' : '') + (e.why ? '&lt;div class="explain"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Neden?&lt;/b&gt;' + e.why + '&lt;/div&gt;' : '');
function checkEx(grade, quiet) {
  const l = LMAP[S.id], i = S.step - 1, e = l.ex[i], k = exKey(l, i); const out = $('#out'); const src = ED.get();
  if (!src.trim() || (e.t === 'query' &amp;&amp; !/EVALUATE[\s\S]*\S/.test(src.replace(/^\s*EVALUATE\s*$/, '')))) { out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="msg info"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Editör boş&lt;/b&gt;Önce bir ifade yaz.&lt;/div&gt;'; return; }
  try {
    if (e.t === 'query') {
      const mine = X.runQuery(measureSet(), src); const html = resultTable(mine.cols, mine.rows);
      if (!grade) { out.innerHTML = html; return; }
      const ref = X.runQuery(measureSet(), e.sol);
      const keyRow = r =&gt; r.map(v =&gt; typeof v === 'number' ? Math.round(v * 1e6) / 1e6 : v instanceof Date ? v.getTime() : v).join('␟');
      const a = mine.rows.map(keyRow).sort(), b = ref.rows.map(keyRow).sort();
      const ok = mine.cols.length === ref.cols.length &amp;&amp; a.length === b.length &amp;&amp; a.every((x, j) =&gt; x === b[j]);
      if (ok) { solved(l, i, k, quiet); out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="msg ok"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Doğru!&lt;/b&gt;Sorgun beklenen ' + ref.rows.length + ' satırı döndürdü.' + explainBox(e) + '&lt;/div&gt;' + html; }
      else out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="msg err"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Henüz değil&lt;/b&gt;' + (mine.cols.length !== ref.cols.length ? 'Beklenen sütun sayısı ' + ref.cols.length + ', seninki ' + mine.cols.length + '.' : 'Beklenen ' + ref.rows.length + ' satır, seninki ' + mine.rows.length + ' satır' + (a.length === b.length ? ' ama değerler farklı.' : '.')) + '&lt;/div&gt;' + html;
      return;
    }
    const ms = measureSet(e.name); const grid = e.grid || DEFAULT_GRID;
    const expr = X.stripDef(src).expr; const err0 = X.check(src); if (err0) throw new X.DaxError(err0);
    const mine = gridEval(ms, expr, grid, e.noTotal);
    if (!grade) { out.innerHTML = gridHTML(mine, null, e.name); return; }
    const ref = gridEval(measureSet(e.name), e.sol, grid, e.noTotal);
    let bad = 0, total = 0;
    ref.forEach((G, gi) =&gt; G.rows.forEach(r =&gt; { total++; const m = (mine[gi].rows.find(x =&gt; X.keyOf(x.k) === X.keyOf(r.k)) || {}).v; if (!cmpVal(m, r.v)) bad++; }));
    if (!bad) { solved(l, i, k, quiet); out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="msg ok"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Doğru!&lt;/b&gt;' + total + ' hücrenin tamamı beklenen sonuçla aynı.' + explainBox(e) + '&lt;/div&gt;' + gridHTML(mine, ref, e.name); }
    else out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="msg err"&gt;&lt;b&gt;Henüz değil&lt;/b&gt;' + total + ' hücreden ' + bad + ' tanesi beklenenden farklı. Farklı hücreler işaretli; “beklenen” değerleri ipucu olarak kullan.&lt;/div&gt;' + gridHTML(mine, ref, e.name);
  } catch (er) {
    if (!er.dax) console.error(er);
    out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="msg err"&gt;&lt;b&gt;DAX hatası&lt;/b&gt;&lt;span class="vend"&gt;' + esc(er.message) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  }
}
function solved(l, i, k, quiet) {
  if (!P.ex[k]) { P.ex[k] = true; addXP(l.ex[i].t === 'mc' ? 5 : 10); if (!quiet) toast('+' + (l.ex[i].t === 'mc' ? 5 : 10) + ' XP'); save(); }
  const b = document.querySelector('.steps button[data-step="' + (i + 1) + '"]'); if (b) b.classList.add('done');
  if (checkLessonDone(l)) { const p = document.querySelector('.pager'); if (p) p.lastElementChild.outerHTML = nextBtn(l); }
}
function gridHTML(mine, ref, name) {
  const f = fmtOf(name);
  return '&lt;div class="panel result"&gt;' + mine.map((G, gi) =&gt; {
    const R = ref &amp;&amp; ref[gi];
    let h = '&lt;div class="rset"&gt;&lt;div class="result-meta"&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + esc(G.g) + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;' + G.rows.length + ' satır&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="rtable"&gt;&lt;table&gt;&lt;thead&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;th&gt;' + (G.g === 'Toplam' ? '' : esc(G.g)) + '&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th class="numc"&gt;Senin sonucun&lt;/th&gt;' + (R ? '&lt;th class="numc"&gt;Beklenen&lt;/th&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;/thead&gt;&lt;tbody&gt;';
    const rows = R ? R.rows : G.rows;
    rows.forEach(r =&gt; { const m = R ? (G.rows.find(x =&gt; X.keyOf(x.k) === X.keyOf(r.k)) || {}).v : r.v; const ok = !R || cmpVal(m, r.v); h += '&lt;tr class="' + (ok ? '' : 'bad') + '"&gt;&lt;td&gt;' + esc(V.fmt(r.k)) + '&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td class="numc"&gt;' + esc(fmtVal(m, f)) + '&lt;/td&gt;' + (R ? '&lt;td class="numc"&gt;' + esc(fmtVal(r.v, f)) + '&lt;/td&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/tr&gt;'; });
    return h + '&lt;/tbody&gt;&lt;/table&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  }).join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;';
}
function fmtVal(v, f) { if (v === null || v === undefined) return '(Boş)'; if (typeof v === 'number') { if (f === 'yuzde') return V.fmt(v, 'yuzde'); if (f === 'para') return V.fmt(v, 'para'); return V.fmt(v); } return V.fmt(v); }
function resultTable(cols, rows, lim) {
  lim = lim || 200;
  let h = '&lt;div class="panel result"&gt;&lt;div class="result-meta"&gt;&lt;span&gt;' + rows.length + ' satır · ' + cols.length + ' sütun&lt;/span&gt;' + (rows.length &gt; lim ? '&lt;span&gt;İlk ' + lim + ' satır gösteriliyor&lt;/span&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="rtable"&gt;&lt;table&gt;&lt;thead&gt;&lt;tr&gt;' + cols.map(c =&gt; '&lt;th&gt;' + esc(c) + '&lt;/th&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;/thead&gt;&lt;tbody&gt;';
  rows.slice(0, lim).forEach(r =&gt; { h += '&lt;tr&gt;' + r.map((v, j) =&gt; { const f = guessFmt(cols[j]); return '&lt;td class="' + (typeof v === 'number' ? 'numc' : '') + (v === null ? ' null' : '') + '"&gt;' + esc(v === null ? '(Boş)' : typeof v === 'number' ? fmtVal(v, f &amp;&amp; /%/.test(cols[j]) ? 'yuzde' : null) : V.fmt(v)) + '&lt;/td&gt;'; }).join('') + '&lt;/tr&gt;'; });
  if (!rows.length) h += '&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td class="null"&gt;Sonuç boş&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;';
  return h + '&lt;/tbody&gt;&lt;/table&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
}

/* ---------- Editör ---------- */
function makeEditor(host, init, onSubmit, isQ) {
  const keys = ['[', ']', '(', ')', '"', ',', '=', '&amp;', '{', '}', 'CALCULATE(', 'SUM(', 'DIVIDE(', 'FILTER(', 'ALL(', 'VAR ', 'RETURN '].concat(isQ ? ['EVALUATE', 'SUMMARIZECOLUMNS('] : []);
  host.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="ed"&gt;&lt;pre aria-hidden="true"&gt;&lt;code&gt;&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;textarea spellcheck="false" autocapitalize="off" autocomplete="off" autocorrect="off" aria-label="DAX editörü" id="daxEditor"&gt;&lt;/textarea&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="ed-keys"&gt;' + keys.map(k =&gt; '&lt;button type="button" data-k="' + esc(k) + '"&gt;' + esc(k.trim()) + '&lt;/button&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;';
  const ta = $('textarea', host), code = $('code', host), pre = $('pre', host);
  const api = { get: () =&gt; ta.value, set: v =&gt; { ta.value = v; paint(); api.onChange &amp;&amp; api.onChange(v); }, onChange: null, focus: () =&gt; ta.focus(), insert: t =&gt; insert(t) };
  function paint() { code.innerHTML = hl(ta.value) + '\n'; pre.scrollTop = ta.scrollTop; pre.scrollLeft = ta.scrollLeft; }
  ta.value = init; paint();
  ta.addEventListener('input', () =&gt; { paint(); api.onChange &amp;&amp; api.onChange(ta.value); });
  ta.addEventListener('scroll', () =&gt; { pre.scrollTop = ta.scrollTop; pre.scrollLeft = ta.scrollLeft; });
  ta.addEventListener('keydown', ev =&gt; {
    if ((ev.ctrlKey || ev.metaKey) &amp;&amp; ev.key === 'Enter') { ev.preventDefault(); onSubmit &amp;&amp; onSubmit(); }
    else if (ev.key === 'Tab' &amp;&amp; !ev.shiftKey) { ev.preventDefault(); insert('    '); }
    else if (ev.key === 'Enter') { const before = ta.value.slice(0, ta.selectionStart); const ln = before.split('\n').pop(); const ind = (ln.match(/^\s*/) || [''])[0] + (/[(,]\s*$/.test(ln) ? '    ' : ''); ev.preventDefault(); insert('\n' + ind); }
  });
  function insert(t) { const s = ta.selectionStart, e = ta.selectionEnd; ta.setRangeText(t, s, e, 'end'); ta.dispatchEvent(new Event('input')); }
  host.querySelectorAll('[data-k]').forEach(b =&gt; b.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; { insert(b.dataset.k); ta.focus(); }));
  return api;
}

/* ---------- Veri Modeli ---------- */
let modelSel = 'Satislar';
const ER_POS = { Departmanlar: [10, 330], Musteriler: [240, 20], Calisanlar: [240, 300], Satislar: [490, 120], Urunler: [750, 20], Tarih: [750, 230], Kategoriler: [1000, 20], StokGorunum: [1000, 120], Hedefler: [1000, 270], WebOlaylari: [1000, 390] };
function viewModel() {
  const params = X.TNAMES.filter(t =&gt; !ER_POS[t]);
  let h = '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;' + head('Veri modeli', 'Anadolu Market anlam modeli', X.TNAMES.length + ' tablo · ' + MODEL.rels.length + ' ilişki. Dört olgu tablosu, yedi boyut ve ' + params.length + ' bağlantısız parametre tablosu.', 'model');
  h += '&lt;div class="panel er" id="er"&gt;' + erSVG() + '&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;div class="panel ptables"&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Bağlantısız (parametre) tablolar&lt;/span&gt;&lt;div class="row" style="gap:6px"&gt;' + params.map(t =&gt; '&lt;button class="chip" data-tbl="' + t + '" aria-pressed="' + (t === modelSel) + '"&gt;' + t + ' &lt;small class="muted"&gt;' + MODEL.tables[t].rows.length + '&lt;/small&gt;&lt;/button&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p class="muted" style="margin:6px 0 0;font-size:.86rem"&gt;Bu tabloların modelde ilişkisi yoktur. Dilimleyici ya da eksen olarak kullanılır; ölçüler seçimi SELECTEDVALUE veya TREATAS ile okur (ne olur parametresi, Top N, para birimi, alan parametresi, RFM segmentleri).&lt;/p&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div id="tdetail"&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;div class="panel" style="padding:18px 20px"&gt;&lt;h3 style="margin-bottom:8px"&gt;Bu modeldeki ilişkiler&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;div class="coltable"&gt;&lt;table&gt;&lt;thead&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;th&gt;Bir (1)&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Çok (*)&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Kardinalite&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Filtre yönü&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Durum&lt;/th&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;/thead&gt;&lt;tbody&gt;' + MODEL.rels.map(r =&gt; '&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;' + r.to + '[' + r.toCol + ']&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;' + r.from + '[' + r.fromCol + ']&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;Bire-çok&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;Tek&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;' + (r.inactive ? '&lt;span class="chip"&gt;Pasif · USERELATIONSHIP&lt;/span&gt;' : 'Etkin') + '&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/tbody&gt;&lt;/table&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  return h + '&lt;/div&gt;';
}
function erSVG() {
  const Wd = 200, rowH = 15, head0 = 24; let lines = '', boxes = '';
  const boxH = t =&gt; head0 + MODEL.tables[t].cols.length * rowH + 6;
  const anchor = (t, c) =&gt; { const [x, y] = ER_POS[t]; const i = MODEL.tables[t].cols.indexOf(c); return [x, y + head0 + i * rowH + rowH / 2 + 1]; };
  MODEL.rels.forEach(r =&gt; {
    if (!ER_POS[r.to] || !ER_POS[r.from]) return;
    const [x1, y1] = anchor(r.to, r.toCol), [x2, y2] = anchor(r.from, r.fromCol); let d, a, b;
    if (Math.abs(x1 - x2) &lt; 10) { a = [x1 + Wd, y1]; b = [x2 + Wd, y2]; const bx = x1 + Wd + 34; d = 'M' + a[0] + ',' + a[1] + ' C' + bx + ',' + a[1] + ' ' + bx + ',' + b[1] + ' ' + b[0] + ',' + b[1]; }
    else { a = x1 &lt; x2 ? [x1 + Wd, y1] : [x1, y1]; b = x1 &lt; x2 ? [x2, y2] : [x2 + Wd, y2]; const mx = (a[0] + b[0]) / 2; d = 'M' + a[0] + ',' + a[1] + ' C' + mx + ',' + a[1] + ' ' + mx + ',' + b[1] + ' ' + b[0] + ',' + b[1]; }
    const right = a[0] &lt;= b[0];
    lines += '&lt;path d="' + d + '"' + (r.inactive ? ' class="inactive"' : '') + '/&gt;&lt;text class="card1" x="' + (a[0] + (right ? 4 : -10)) + '" y="' + (a[1] - 3) + '"&gt;1&lt;/text&gt;&lt;text class="card1" x="' + (b[0] + (right &amp;&amp; Math.abs(x1 - x2) &gt;= 10 ? -10 : 4)) + '" y="' + (b[1] - 3) + '"&gt;*&lt;/text&gt;';
  });
  Object.keys(ER_POS).forEach(t =&gt; { const [x, y] = ER_POS[t]; const tb = MODEL.tables[t]; const fact = tb.kind === 'fact'; boxes += '&lt;g class="tbox' + (t === modelSel ? ' sel' : '') + (fact ? ' fact' : '') + '" data-tbl="' + t + '" tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="' + t + ' tablosu"&gt;&lt;rect x="' + x + '" y="' + y + '" width="' + Wd + '" height="' + boxH(t) + '" rx="8"/&gt;&lt;path class="hd" d="M' + x + ',' + (y + head0) + 'V' + (y + 8) + 'Q' + x + ',' + y + ' ' + (x + 8) + ',' + y + 'H' + (x + Wd - 8) + 'Q' + (x + Wd) + ',' + y + ' ' + (x + Wd) + ',' + (y + 8) + 'V' + (y + head0) + 'Z"/&gt;&lt;text class="t" x="' + (x + 10) + '" y="' + (y + 16) + '"&gt;' + t + '&lt;/text&gt;&lt;text class="k" x="' + (x + Wd - 10) + '" y="' + (y + 16) + '" text-anchor="end"&gt;' + (fact ? 'OLGU' : 'BOYUT') + '&lt;/text&gt;' + tb.cols.map((c, i) =&gt; { const isPk = MODEL.rels.some(r =&gt; r.to === t &amp;&amp; r.toCol === c), isFk = MODEL.rels.some(r =&gt; r.from === t &amp;&amp; r.fromCol === c); return '&lt;text x="' + (x + 10) + '" y="' + (y + head0 + i * rowH + 12) + '"&gt;' + c + '&lt;/text&gt;' + (isPk ? '&lt;text class="k" x="' + (x + Wd - 10) + '" y="' + (y + head0 + i * rowH + 12) + '" text-anchor="end"&gt;1&lt;/text&gt;' : isFk ? '&lt;text class="fk" x="' + (x + Wd - 10) + '" y="' + (y + head0 + i * rowH + 12) + '" text-anchor="end"&gt;*&lt;/text&gt;' : ''); }).join('') + '&lt;/g&gt;'; });
  return '&lt;svg viewBox="0 0 1240 560" role="img" aria-label="Model diyagramı"&gt;' + lines + boxes + '&lt;/svg&gt;';
}
function mountModel() { showTable(modelSel); }
function showTable(t) {
  modelSel = t; const er = $('#er'); if (er) er.innerHTML = erSVG();
  document.querySelectorAll('.ptables [data-tbl]').forEach(b =&gt; b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.dataset.tbl === t));
  const tb = MODEL.tables[t]; const rows = tb.rows.slice(0, 8);
  let h = '&lt;div class="panel" style="padding:18px 20px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:12px"&gt;&lt;div class="row" style="justify-content:space-between"&gt;&lt;h2&gt;' + t + '&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;span class="chip"&gt;' + tb.rows.length.toLocaleString('tr-TR') + ' satır · ' + ({ fact: 'olgu', dim: 'boyut', param: 'parametre (bağlantısız)' })[tb.kind] + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p class="muted" style="margin:0"&gt;' + esc((MODEL.notes || {})[t] || '') + '&lt;/p&gt;';
  h += '&lt;div class="coltable"&gt;&lt;table&gt;&lt;thead&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;th&gt;Sütun&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Tür&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Açıklama&lt;/th&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;/thead&gt;&lt;tbody&gt;' + tb.cols.map(c =&gt; { const r0 = tb.rows.find(r =&gt; r[c] != null); const v = r0 ? r0[c] : null; const ty = v instanceof Date ? 'Tarih' : typeof v === 'number' ? (Number.isInteger(v) ? 'Tam sayı' : 'Ondalık') : 'Metin'; const calc = tb.calc &amp;&amp; tb.calc[c]; return '&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;' + c + '&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;' + ty + (calc ? ' &lt;span class="chip"&gt;hesaplanmış&lt;/span&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;' + esc((MODEL.colDesc || {})[t + '[' + c + ']'] || '') + (calc ? '&lt;code class="mcode"&gt;' + hl(calc) + '&lt;/code&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;'; }).join('') + '&lt;/tbody&gt;&lt;/table&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  h += '&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;İlk ' + rows.length + ' satır&lt;/span&gt;' + resultTable(tb.cols, rows.map(r =&gt; tb.cols.map(c =&gt; r[c] === undefined ? null : r[c])));
  h += '&lt;div class="row"&gt;&lt;button class="btn sm" data-try="EVALUATE TOPN(20, ' + t + ')"&gt;Stüdyoda sorgula&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  $('#tdetail').innerHTML = h;
}

'&lt;button class="btn" data-act="st-save"&gt;Ölçü olarak kaydet&lt;/button&gt;' : '') + '&lt;button class="btn ghost sm" data-act="st-clear"&gt;Temizle&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';

function sideList() {
  if (ST.side === 'fields') return X.TNAMES.map(t =&gt; '&lt;details' + (t === 'Satislar' ? ' open' : '') + '&gt;&lt;summary&gt;' + t + '&lt;span&gt;' + MODEL.tables[t].rows.length + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/summary&gt;&lt;ul&gt;' + MODEL.tables[t].cols.map(c =&gt; '&lt;li&gt;&lt;button data-ins="' + esc(t + '[' + c + ']') + '"&gt;' + c + '&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/li&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;/details&gt;').join('') + '&lt;p class="muted" style="font-size:.78rem;margin:8px 4px 0"&gt;Bir alana dokununca editöre eklenir.&lt;/p&gt;';
  const groups = {}; LIB.forEach(m =&gt; (groups[m.group] = groups[m.group] || []).push(m));
  let h = '';
  if ((P.mine || []).length) h += '&lt;details open&gt;&lt;summary&gt;Ölçülerim&lt;span&gt;' + P.mine.length + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/summary&gt;&lt;ul&gt;' + P.mine.map((m, i) =&gt; '&lt;li class="mm"&gt;&lt;button data-ins="[' + esc(m.name) + ']" title="' + esc(m.expr) + '"&gt;' + esc(m.name) + '&lt;/button&gt;&lt;button class="x" data-act="mine-edit" data-i="' + i + '" aria-label="Düzenle"&gt;✎&lt;/button&gt;&lt;button class="x" data-act="mine-del" data-i="' + i + '" aria-label="Sil"&gt;✕&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/li&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;/details&gt;';
  Object.keys(groups).forEach(g =&gt; { h += '&lt;details' + (g === 'Temel' ? ' open' : '') + '&gt;&lt;summary&gt;' + g + '&lt;span&gt;' + groups[g].length + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/summary&gt;&lt;ul&gt;' + groups[g].map(m =&gt; '&lt;li&gt;&lt;button data-ins="[' + esc(m.name) + ']" title="' + esc(m.name + ' = ' + m.expr) + '"&gt;' + esc(m.name) + '&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/li&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/ul&gt;&lt;/details&gt;'; });
  return h + '&lt;p class="muted" style="font-size:.78rem;margin:8px 4px 0"&gt;Hazır ölçüler dashboardlarda kullanılır. Üzerine gelince formülü görünür; dokununca editöre eklenir.&lt;/p&gt;';
}
function mountStudio() { SED = makeEditor($('#sed'), ST.code[ST.mode] || '', studioRun, ST.mode === 'q'); SED.onChange = v =&gt; { ST.code[ST.mode] = v; saveStudio(); }; studioRun(); }
function upsertMine(name, expr, quiet) {
  P.mine = P.mine || []; const i = P.mine.findIndex(m =&gt; m.name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase());
  if (LIBMAP[name]) { if (!quiet) toast('“' + name + '” hazır bir ölçünün adı; başka bir ad seç.'); return false; }
  if (i &gt;= 0) P.mine[i].expr = expr; else P.mine.push({ name, expr }); save(); if (!quiet) toast('Kaydedildi: [' + name + ']'); return true;
}
const VC = {}; ['column', 'bar', 'line', 'donut'].forEach(n =&gt; { VC[n] = (d, o) =&gt; V[n](d, Object.assign({ W: CW }, o || {})); });
function studioRun() {
  const out = $('#stOut'); if (!out) return; const src = SED ? SED.get() : ST.code[ST.mode];
  if (!src.trim()) { out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="panel empty"&gt;Bir ifade yaz ya da yukarıdan bir örnek yükle.&lt;/div&gt;'; return; }
  const t0 = performance.now(); CW = studioW();
  try {
    if (ST.mode === 'q') { const r = X.runQuery(measureSet(), src); out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="timing"&gt;' + Math.round(performance.now() - t0) + ' ms&lt;/div&gt;' + resultTable(r.cols, r.rows); return; }
    const d = X.stripDef(src); const err0 = X.check(src); if (err0) throw new X.DaxError(err0);
    const name = d.name || 'Ölçü'; const ms = measureSet(d.name); const f = fmtOf(name);
    const sl = []; if (ST.yil.length) sl.push({ t: 'Tarih', c: 'Yil', values: ST.yil }); if (ST.kanal.length) sl.push({ t: 'Satislar', c: 'Kanal', values: ST.kanal });
    if (ST.chart === 'card') { const v = X.evalMeasure(ms, d.expr, sl); out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="timing"&gt;' + Math.round(performance.now() - t0) + ' ms&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="panel vbox" style="max-width:320px"&gt;' + V.card(v, name, f) + '&lt;/div&gt;'; return; }
    const a1 = parseCol(ST.axis), a2 = ST.axis2 ? parseCol(ST.axis2) : null;
    if (a2) {
      const r = X.query({ rows: [a1, a2], measures: ms, values: [d.expr], slicers: sl, totals: true });
      const k1 = [], k2 = []; r.rows.forEach(x =&gt; { if (!k1.some(v =&gt; X.keyOf(v) === X.keyOf(x.keys[0]))) k1.push(x.keys[0]); if (!k2.some(v =&gt; X.keyOf(v) === X.keyOf(x.keys[1]))) k2.push(x.keys[1]); });
      k2.sort((a, b) =&gt; X.sortCompare(a2, a, b));
      const cell = (a, b) =&gt; { const x = r.rows.find(z =&gt; X.keyOf(z.keys[0]) === X.keyOf(a) &amp;&amp; X.keyOf(z.keys[1]) === X.keyOf(b)); return x ? x.vals[0] : null; };
      const rowTot = k1.map(a =&gt; X.evalMeasure(ms, d.expr, sl.filter(s =&gt; !(s.t === a1.t &amp;&amp; s.c === a1.c)).concat([{ t: a1.t, c: a1.c, values: [a] }]), { shadow: sl }));
      const colTot = k2.map(b =&gt; X.evalMeasure(ms, d.expr, sl.filter(s =&gt; !(s.t === a2.t &amp;&amp; s.c === a2.c)).concat([{ t: a2.t, c: a2.c, values: [b] }]), { shadow: sl }));
      const rows = k1.map((a, i) =&gt; [V.fmt(a)].concat(k2.map(b =&gt; cell(a, b))).concat([rowTot[i]]));
      out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="timing"&gt;' + r.rows.length + ' hücre · ' + Math.round(performance.now() - t0) + ' ms&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="panel vbox"&gt;&lt;div class="vtitle"&gt;' + esc(name) + ' · ' + esc(a1.c) + ' × ' + esc(a2.c) + '&lt;/div&gt;' + V.table([a1.c].concat(k2.map(b =&gt; V.fmt(b))).concat(['Toplam']), rows, { fmts: [null].concat(k2.map(() =&gt; f)).concat([f]), cf: Object.fromEntries(k2.map((b, i) =&gt; [i + 1, 'heat'])), total: ['Toplam'].concat(colTot).concat(r.total) }) + '&lt;/div&gt;';
      bindMarks(); return;
    }
    const r = X.query({ rows: [a1], measures: ms, values: [d.expr], slicers: sl, totals: true });
    const cats = r.rows.map(x =&gt; V.fmt(x.keys[0])), vals = r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[0]);
    const numeric = vals.every(v =&gt; v === null || typeof v === 'number');
    let vis = '';
    if (!numeric || ST.chart === 'table' || !r.rows.length) vis = V.table([a1.c, name], r.rows.map(x =&gt; [V.fmt(x.keys[0]), x.vals[0]]), { fmts: [null, f], cf: numeric ? { 1: 'bar' } : null, total: ['Toplam', r.total[0]] });
    else {
      const data = { cats, series: [{ name, vals }], fmt: f };
      vis = ST.chart === 'bar' ? VC.bar(data) : ST.chart === 'line' ? VC.line(data, { area: true }) : ST.chart === 'donut' ? VC.donut(data) : VC.column(data, { labels: cats.length &lt;= 14 });
      vis += '&lt;div class="vtot"&gt;&lt;span&gt;Toplam satırı&lt;/span&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + esc(fmtVal(r.total[0], f)) + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
      vis += '&lt;details class="vdet"&gt;&lt;summary&gt;Matris olarak gör&lt;/summary&gt;' + V.table([a1.c, name], r.rows.map(x =&gt; [V.fmt(x.keys[0]), x.vals[0]]), { fmts: [null, f], total: ['Toplam', r.total[0]] }) + '&lt;/details&gt;';
    }
    out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="timing"&gt;' + r.rows.length + ' satır · ' + Math.round(performance.now() - t0) + ' ms' + (!numeric &amp;&amp; ST.chart !== 'table' ? ' · metin sonuçlar matris olarak gösterildi' : '') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="panel vbox"&gt;&lt;div class="vtitle"&gt;' + esc(name) + ' · ' + esc(a1.c) + (sl.length ? ' &lt;span class="muted"&gt;(' + sl.map(s =&gt; s.values.join(', ')).join(' · ') + ')&lt;/span&gt;' : '') + '&lt;/div&gt;' + vis + '&lt;/div&gt;';
    bindMarks();
  } catch (er) {
    if (!er.dax) console.error(er);
    out.innerHTML = '&lt;div class="msg err"&gt;&lt;b&gt;DAX hatası&lt;/b&gt;&lt;span class="vend"&gt;' + esc(er.message) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';
  }
}

/* ---------- Formüller ---------- */
const fq = { cat: 'Tümü', q: '' };
function viewFormulas() {
  const cats = ['Tümü'].concat([...new Set(window.PBI_CATALOG.map(x =&gt; x[1]))]);
  return '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;' + head('Başvuru', 'DAX formülleri', window.PBI_CATALOG.length + ' fonksiyon. Her örnek bu modelde çalışır: “Stüdyoda dene” ile sonucu gör.', 'formulas') + '&lt;div class="row"&gt;&lt;input class="input" id="fQ" placeholder="Fonksiyon ara: CALCULATE, pencere, tarih…" value="' + esc(fq.q) + '" style="max-width:340px" aria-label="Fonksiyon ara"&gt;&lt;div class="filters"&gt;' + cats.map(c =&gt; '&lt;button data-fcat="' + esc(c) + '" aria-pressed="' + (fq.cat === c) + '"&gt;' + esc(c) + '&lt;/button&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="fgrid" id="fList"&gt;' + fList() + '&lt;/div&gt;' + libSection() + '&lt;/div&gt;';
}
function fList() {
  const q = fq.q.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('tr');
  const items = window.PBI_CATALOG.filter(x =&gt; (fq.cat === 'Tümü' || x[1] === fq.cat) &amp;&amp; (!q || (x.join(' ')).toLocaleLowerCase('tr').includes(q)));
  if (!items.length) return '&lt;div class="panel empty"&gt;Aramana uyan fonksiyon yok.&lt;/div&gt;';
  return items.map(([n, c, syn, d, ex]) =&gt; '&lt;article class="panel fcard"&gt;&lt;div class="row" style="justify-content:space-between"&gt;&lt;h3&gt;' + esc(n) + '&lt;/h3&gt;&lt;span class="chip"&gt;' + esc(c) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;code class="syn"&gt;' + esc(syn) + '&lt;/code&gt;&lt;p&gt;' + esc(d) + '&lt;/p&gt;&lt;pre class="dax ex"' + (/^(--|\/\/)/.test(ex) ? ' data-notry' : '') + '&gt;' + esc(ex) + '&lt;/pre&gt;&lt;/article&gt;').join('');
}
function libSection() {
  const lv = m =&gt; m.lv || (window.PBI_PATTERNS || []).find(p =&gt; p.name === m.name)?.lv || '';
  return '&lt;section class="lvl-block"&gt;&lt;div class="sec-head"&gt;&lt;div&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Hazır ölçüler&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h2&gt;Bu modelin ölçü kütüphanesi (' + LIB.length + ')&lt;/h2&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;button class="btn sm" data-go="lab"&gt;Uzun kalıplar: Ölçü Laboratuvarı&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;p class="muted" style="margin:0;max-width:70ch"&gt;Dashboardlar ve alıştırmalar bu ölçüleri kullanır. İyi bir model ölçüleri birbirinin üzerine kurar: temel ölçüler bir kez tanımlanır, diğerleri onları çağırır.&lt;/p&gt;&lt;div class="panel"&gt;&lt;div class="coltable"&gt;&lt;table&gt;&lt;thead&gt;&lt;tr&gt;&lt;th&gt;Ölçü&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Seviye&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Formül&lt;/th&gt;&lt;th&gt;Açıklama&lt;/th&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;&lt;/thead&gt;&lt;tbody&gt;' + LIB.map(m =&gt; '&lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;[' + esc(m.name) + ']&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;' + (lv(m) ? lvBadge(lv(m)) : '') + '&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;code class="mcode"&gt;' + hl(m.expr.length &gt; 420 ? m.expr.slice(0, 420) + '\n…' : m.expr) + '&lt;/code&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;' + esc(m.desc || '') + '&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;').join('') + '&lt;/tbody&gt;&lt;/table&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/section&gt;';
}

/* ---------- Seviye testi ---------- */
let T = null;
function viewTest() {
  const Q2 = window.PBI_PLACEMENT;
  if (!T) T = { i: 0, score: {}, ans: [] };
  if (T.i &gt;= Q2.length) {
    let lv = null; for (const L of LEVELS) { const n = Q2.filter(q =&gt; q.lv === L.id).length; if (n &amp;&amp; (T.score[L.id] || 0) &gt;= Math.ceil(n * 0.75)) lv = L.id; else break; }
    P.placement = lv; save();
    const total = T.ans.filter(Boolean).length; const nextL = LEVELS[lv ? LVI(lv) + 1 : 0];
    const rec = !lv || lv === 'A1' ? 'profesyonel' : lv === 'A2' || lv === 'B1' ? 'analist' : lv === 'B2' ? 'pl300' : 'gelistirici';
    const start = nextL ? LESSONS.find(l =&gt; l.lvl === nextL.id) : null;
    return '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;&lt;div class="panel hero-main"&gt;&lt;span class="eyebrow"&gt;Sonuç&lt;/span&gt;&lt;h1&gt;' + total + '/' + Q2.length + ' doğru · &lt;em&gt;' + (lv ? lv + ' · ' + lvName(lv) : 'Başlangıç') + '&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/h1&gt;&lt;p&gt;' + (lv ? lv + ' seviyesini geçtin.       '&lt;div class="panel lessons"&gt;' + LEVELS.map(L =&gt; { const n = Q2.filter(q =&gt; q.lv === L.id).length; const s = T.score[L.id] || 0; return '&lt;div class="litem" style="cursor:default"&gt;&lt;span&gt;' + lvBadge(L.id) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;&lt;b&gt;' + L.name + '&lt;/b&gt;&lt;small&gt;' + s + '/' + n + ' doğru · ' + (s &gt;= Math.ceil(n * 0.75) ?   }
  const q = Q2[T.i];
  return '&lt;div class="wrap"&gt;' + head('Seviye testi · ' + (T.i + 1) + '/' + Q2.length, 'Nereden başlamalısın?', null, 'test') + '&lt;div class="bar"&gt;&lt;i style="width:' + (T.i / Q2.length * 100) + '%"&gt;&lt;/i&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="panel brief" style="max-width:760px"&gt;' + lvBadge(q.lv) + '&lt;div class="q"&gt;' + esc(q.q) + '&lt;/div&gt;&lt;div class="mc"&gt;' + q.o.map((o, i) =&gt; '&lt;button data-tq="' + i + '"&gt;&lt;span class="key"&gt;' + String.fromCharCode(65 + i) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;' + esc(o) + '&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/button&gt;').join('') + '&lt;button data-tq="-1"&gt;&lt;span class="key"&gt;?&lt;/span&gt;&lt;span&gt;Bilmiyorum&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/button&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;&lt;/div&gt;';

function fromHash() {
  const h = decodeURIComponent((location.hash || '').slice(1));
  const i = h.indexOf('-'); const pre = i &lt; 0 ? h : h.slice(0, i); const id = i &lt; 0 ? null : h.slice(i + 1);
  const v = Object.keys(VIEWS).find(k =&gt; k !== 'home' &amp;&amp; (VIEWS[k].hash || k) === pre);
  if (!v) { S = { view: 'home', id: null, step: 0, ctx: null }; return; }
  if (v === 'lesson' &amp;&amp; !LMAP[id]) { S = { view: 'curr', id: null, step: 0 }; return; }
  S = { view: v, id, step: 0, ctx: v === 'path' ? id : null };
}
function start() {
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', () =&gt; { if (location.hash === lastHash) return; lastHash = location.hash; fromHash(); render(); window.scrollTo(0, 0); });
  let rz = null; window.addEventListener('resize', () =&gt; { clearTimeout(rz); rz = setTimeout(() =&gt; { const d = VIEWS[S.view]; if (d &amp;&amp; d.resize) d.resize(); }, 250); });
  fromHash(); render();
}

})();

&lt;parameter name="content"&gt;/* PBIPatika sayfaları: Görseller (biçim bölmesi benzetimi), Dashboardlar, Ölçü Laboratuvarı, Püf Noktaları, Menüler, Final. */
(function () {
'use strict';
const C = window.PBI_CORE, X = C.X, V = C.V, MODEL = C.MODEL, esc = C.esc, $ = C.$;
const DC = window.PBI_DASHCORE, FP = window.PBI_FORMAT;
const pc = C.parseCol;
const Y24 = [{ t: 'Tarih', c: 'Yil', values: [2024] }];
const MS = () =&gt; C.measureSet();
const fmtOf = n =&gt; C.fmtOf(n);
const sortRows = (r, how) =&gt; { if (how === 'valueDesc') r.rows.sort((a, b) =&gt; (b.vals[0] || 0) - (a.vals[0] || 0)); else if (how === 'valueAsc') r.rows.sort((a, b) =&gt; (a.vals[0] || 0) - (b.vals[0] || 0)); return r; };
const cats = r =&gt; r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.keys[0]);
const col = (r, j) =&gt; r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[j]);
const uniqK = (r, i, colRef) =&gt; { const out = []; r.rows.forEach(x =&gt; { if (!out.some(v =&gt; X.keyOf(v) === X.keyOf(x.keys[i]))) out.push(x.keys[i]); }); if (colRef) out.sort((a, b) =&gt; X.sortCompare(colRef, a, b)); return out; };
const pick = (r, a, b, j) =&gt; { const x = r.rows.find(z =&gt; X.keyOf(z.keys[0]) === X.keyOf(a) &amp;&amp; X.keyOf(z.keys[1]) === X.keyOf(b)); return x ? x.vals[j || 0] : null; };
const pctS = v =&gt; v == null ? '—' : (v &gt;= 0 ? '+' : '−') + Math.round(Math.abs(v) * 100) + '%';
const later = fn =&gt; setTimeout(fn, 16);

/* ======================================================================
   GÖRSELLER: Görselleştirmeler bölmesi benzetimi
   ====================================================================== */
const VS = { id: 'column', w: {}, f: {}, a: {}, g: {}, tab: 'build', last: null, sl: [], dpath: [] };
function vsDefaults(id) {
  const def = FP.visuals[id]; Object.assign(VS, { id, w: {}, f: {}, a: {}, g: {}, last: null, sl: [], dpath: [] });
  if (!def) return;
  def.wells.forEach(w =&gt; VS.w[w.k] = w.d);
  def.fmt.forEach(s =&gt; s.i.forEach(it =&gt; { if (it.t !== 'info') VS.f[it.k] = it.d; }));
  (def.an || []).forEach(it =&gt; VS.a[it.k] = it.d);
  FP.G.forEach(it =&gt; { if (it.t !== 'info') VS.g[it.k] = it.d; });
}
vsDefaults('column');
const simW = () =&gt; { const ww = window.innerWidth; const pane = ww &gt; 1080 ? 380 : 0; return Math.max(270, Math.min(760, C.mainW() - pane - 70)); };
const slFor = cols =&gt; cols.some(c =&gt; /^Tarih\[(Yil|YilAy|YilCeyrek)\]$/.test(c)) ? [] : Y24;
const qq = (cols, vals, sl) =&gt; X.query({ rows: cols.map(pc), measures: MS(), values: vals, slicers: sl || slFor(cols), totals: true });
const label = k =&gt; (k.match(/\[(.+)\]$/) || [0, k])[1];
function tipFor(colRef, ks, sl) {
  const c = pc(colRef); const ms = MS();
  return i =&gt; { const f = (sl || []).concat([{ t: c.t, c: c.c, values: [ks[i]] }]); const r = X.query({ rows: [pc('Tarih[YilAy]')], measures: ms, values: ['Net Satış'], slicers: [{ t: c.t, c: c.c, values: [ks[i]] }], totals: false }); const yoy = X.evalMeasure(ms, '[YoY %]', f);
    return { title: 'Araç ipucu sayfası · ' + ks[i], cards: [['Net Satış', V.fmt(X.evalMeasure(ms, '[Net Satış]', f), 'para', true)], ['Geçen yıla göre', pctS(yoy)], ['Kâr marjı', V.fmt(X.evalMeasure(ms, '[Kâr Marjı %]', f), 'yuzde')]], spark: r.rows.map(x =&gt; x.vals[0]).slice(-12), sparkLabel: 'Son 12 ay net satış' }; };
}
function baseOpts() {
  const f = VS.f, a = VS.a; const o = { W: simW(), labels: !!f.labels, legend: f.legend || 'auto', grid: f.grid !== false, yMin: f.yMin || 'zero', units: f.units &amp;&amp; f.units !== 'auto' ? f.units : undefined, colorMode: f.colorMode || 'series', lines: [] };
  if (f.gap) o.gap = +f.gap;
  if (a.avg) o.lines.push({ type: 'avg' }); if (a.median) o.lines.push({ type: 'median' }); if (a.minmax) o.lines.push({ type: 'min' }, { type: 'max' }); if (a.trend) o.lines.push({ type: 'trend' });
  if (a.forecast &amp;&amp; a.forecast !== '0') o.forecast = +a.forecast;
  return o;
}
function constLine(o, xcol, vals, sl) { if (!VS.a.const) return; let t = null; try { const r = qq([xcol], ['Hedef'], sl); const hv = col(r, 0).filter(v =&gt; v != null); if (hv.length) t = hv.reduce((p, q) =&gt; p + q, 0) / hv.length; } catch (e) {} if (t == null) { const v = vals.filter(x =&gt; x != null); t = v.length ? v.reduce((p, q) =&gt; p + q, 0) / v.length * 1.1 : 0; } o.lines.push({ type: 'const', value: t, label: 'Hedef' }); }
const SIM = {
  column: () =&gt; barLike('column'), bar: () =&gt; barLike('bar'),
  stacked: () =&gt; { const w = VS.w, r = qq([w.x, w.leg], [w.y]); const xs = uniqK(r, 0, pc(w.x)), ls = uniqK(r, 1, pc(w.leg)); const o = baseOpts(); o.stacked = true; return { m: [w.y], html: V.column({ cats: xs.map(String), series: ls.map(l =&gt; ({ name: String(l), vals: xs.map(x =&gt; pick(r, x, l)) })), fmt: fmtOf(w.y) }, o), title: w.y + ' · ' + label(w.x) + ' ve ' + label(w.leg) }; },
  pct: () =&gt; { const w = VS.w, r = qq([w.x, w.leg], [w.y]); const xs = uniqK(r, 0, pc(w.x)), ls = uniqK(r, 1, pc(w.leg)); const o = baseOpts(); o.pct = true; return { m: [w.y], html: V.bar({ cats: xs.map(String), series: ls.map(l =&gt; ({ name: String(l), vals: xs.map(x =&gt; pick(r, x, l)) })), fmt: fmtOf(w.y) }, o), title: label(w.x) + ' başına ' + label(w.leg) + ' karışımı (%100)' }; },
  line: () =&gt; lineLike(false), area: () =&gt; lineLike(true),
  combo: () =&gt; { const w = VS.w, r = qq([w.x], [w.y, w.y2]); const o = baseOpts(); constLine(o, w.x, col(r, 0)); return { m: [w.y, w.y2], html: V.column({ cats: cats(r).map(String), series: [{ name: w.y, vals: col(r, 0) }, { name: w.y2, type: 'line', vals: col(r, 1), color: 'var(--s2)' }], fmt: fmtOf(w.y) }, o), title: w.y + ' ve ' + w.y2 }; },
  donut: () =&gt; { const w = VS.w, f = VS.f, r = qq([w.x], [w.y]); const ks = cats(r); return { m: [w.y], html: V.donut({ cats: ks, series: [{ name: w.y, vals: col(r, 0) }], fmt: fmtOf(w.y) }, { W: Math.min(simW(), 460), inner: +f.inner, labelMode: f.labelMode, legend: f.legend, tipx: VS.g.tooltip === 'page' ? tipFor(w.x, ks, Y24) : null }), title: label(w.x) + ' payı · 2024' }; },
  treemap: () =&gt; { const w = VS.w, f = VS.f; const isU = w.x === 'Urunler[Urun]'; const r = sortRows(qq(isU ?
